Let's code helper `fast_conv_max(a)`:
```
def fast_conv_max(a):
    n = a.size
    if n == 0: return 0.0
    if n < 64:
       return float(np.max(np.convolve(a,a))) # direct small exact
    length = 1
    while length < 2*n-1: length <<=1
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=length)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=length)
    # Due to FFT, maybe small imaginary discarded.
    return float(np.max(c[:2*n-1]))
```
Multiplying `fa*fa` not conj. For real a, c real but irfft assumes Hermitian; `fa*fa` may not have exact Hermitian symmetry due to roundoff. `irfft` uses real part. It should be okay; small imaginary not. Alternatively use `np.fft.ifft(fa*fa).real`. `irfft` faster. Need ensure no large negative. We can `c = np.maximum(c,0)`? But if numerical error negative where true 0, max unaffected; if tiny negative in positive? no. Not needed.

Algorithm for parameter search:
```
def family_sequence(n, alpha, beta, gamma=0):
  q = get_q(n) (cache)
  x = arange(n)/max(n-1,1)
  p = 1 + alpha*x
  if beta !=0: p += beta * x*(1-x)
  if gamma !=0: p += gamma * x*x*(1-x)?
  p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
  a = q * p
  if max(a) <=0: return None
  return a / max(a)
```

Let's outline final code:

```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

_Q_CACHE = {}

def get_q(n):
    if n in _Q_CACHE: return _Q_CACHE[n].copy()
    q=np.empty(n,float)
    if n==1: q[0]=1.0
    else:
       q[0]=1.0
       idx=np.arange(1,n)
       # recurrence ratio q_i/q_{i-1} = (2i-1)/(2i)
       ratios = (2*idx -1)/(2*idx)
       q[1:] = np.cumprod(ratios)
    _Q_CACHE[n]=q.copy()
    return q.copy()
```
Check recurrence for i=1: (2*1-1)/(2*1)=1/2. Good.

```
def conv_max_fast(a):
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return 0.0
   if n <= 128:
       return float(np.max(np.convolve(a,a)))
   L=1
   m=2*n-1
   while L<m: L <<=1
   F=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(F*F, L)
   # To reduce possible negative tiny, no.
   return float(np.max(c[:m]))

def score_fast(a):
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return np.inf
   s=float(np.sum(a))
   if s <= 0.0: return np.inf
   mb=conv_max_fast(a)
   if mb <0: mb=0
   return float(2.0*n*mb/(s*s))

def make_family(n, alpha, beta):
   q=get_q(n)
   x=np.arange(n, dtype=float)/float(max(n-1,1))
   p=1.0 + alpha*x
   if beta !=0.0:
      p = p + beta*x*(1.0-x)
      # maybe three-term?
   # ensure nonnegative p; if slight negative due to roundoff clip, if severely negative maybe clip too
   p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
   a=q*p
   mx=float(np.max(a))
   if mx <=0 or not np.isfinite(mx): return None
   a = a/mx
   return a

def score_family(n, alpha, beta=0.0):
   a=make_family(n, alpha, beta)
   if a is None: return np.inf
   return score_fast(a)
```
But `x*(1-x)` nonnegative, so if beta negative beyond -4/alpha? p(0.5) maybe. We can clip.

def build_conv_indices(n):
    col = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32)
    m = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int32)
    cols = np.repeat(col, n, axis=0) # length n*n, each col repeated n
    offsets = np.tile(m, n) # m for each block
    rows = (cols + offsets).astype(np.int32)
    return rows, cols, offsets
```

Detailed LP function for dense:
```
def solve_lp_for_f(f, itertag=0):
    n=f.size
    M=conv_max_fast(f)
    if M<=0 or not finite: return None, M
    h=f/M
    A=np.empty((2*n-1, n), dtype=float)
    # Need fill with h[k-j]
    for k in range(2*n-1):
        lo = max(0, k-n+1)
        hi = min(n, k+1)
        # m = k - j for j in [lo,hi]
        m = k - np.arange(lo, hi)
        A[k, lo:hi] = h[m]
    res = linprog(c=-np.ones(n), A_ub=A, b_ub=np.ones(2*n-1), bounds=[(0,None)]*n, method='highs', options={'time_limit':10})
    if not res.success: maybe return None
    g=np.asarray(res.x).clip(0,None)
    s=g.sum()
    if s<=0: return None
    return g/s, M
```

def build_idx(n):
    idx = np.zeros((2*n-1, n), dtype=np.int16 if n<32767)
    for k in range(2*n-1):
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n,k+1)
       idx[k, lo:hi] = k - np.arange(lo, hi)
    return idx
```

Local search final:
```
def local_search(a, rng, deadline, max_blocks=...):
   a = a / max(a) # max normalization? Use sum normalization? Score independent. Keep max norm to avoid large sum? Use max1.
   score=score_fast(a)
   for it in range until deadline:
      # choose perturbation mode
      cand=a.copy()
      # block log
      L=int(rng.integers(max(2,n//200), max(3,n//20)))
      st=int(rng.integers(0,n-L+1))
      cand[st:st+L] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma, size=L))
      # random symmetric symmetrize? Maybe sometimes cand=0.5*(cand+cand[::-1])
      # clip nonneg, scale max1
      cand=np.clip(cand,0,None); cand=cand/np.max(cand) if max>0
      sc=score_fast(cand)
      if sc < score - 1e-12: accept, sigma maybe same; else sigma*=1.1 up to 0.5? 
   return best

def build_matrix_info(n):
    idx = np.full((2*n-1,n), -1, dtype=np.int16)
    for k in range(2*n-1):
       lo=max(0,k-n+1); hi=min(n,k+1)
       if hi>lo: idx[k,lo:hi]=k-np.arange(lo,hi) # dtype int64 assign to int16
    valid = idx >=0
    safe = idx.copy(); safe[~valid]=0
    return valid, safe
```

Implementation details for LP stage function:
```
def lp_optimize(f, n, info, max_iters, rng, deadline, verbose=False):
    current = f.copy(); current/=sum
    cur_M = conv_max_fast(current); cur_score=2*n*cur_M
    best=current.copy(); best_M=cur_M
    stagnation=0
    for it in range(max_iters):
       if time.monotonic()>deadline: break
       g, M = solve_lp_dense(current, info)
       if g is None: 
          # reduce perturb? break
          break
       # line search
       best_f=current; best_M=cur_M
       for t in (0.2,0.5,0.8,1.0):
          f_new=(1-t)*current + t*g
          f_new=np.maximum(f_new, 1e-15); f_new/=f_new.sum()
          M_new=conv_max_fast(f_new)
          if M_new < best_M:
             best_M=M_new; best_f=f_new
       if best_M < cur_M*(1-1e-10):
          current=best_f; cur_M=best_M
          if cur_M < best_M_global: update global
          stagnation=0
       else:
          stagnation+=1
          if stagnation >=4:
             # perturb current
             sigma=0.25
             current = current * np.exp(rng.normal(0, sigma, size=n))
             current=np.maximum(current,1e-15); current/=current.sum()
             cur_M=conv_max_fast(current)
             stagnation=0
    return best_global
```
Need max_iters based on time. We can do while time<deadline. Limit LP solves to e.g., 200 for n1, 80 for n2. Each solve time unknown; check before.

       if sc<best_score: update
    # coordinate refine best 200 steps

def local_search(a, rng, deadline, label):
  best=a.copy(); best_score=score_fast(best)
  sigma=0.1
  stagn=0
  it=0
  while time.monotonic()<deadline:
     cand=best.copy()
     n=cand.size
     if rng.random()<0.7:
        L=int(rng.integers(max(2,n//200), max(3,n//20)))
        st=int(rng.integers(0,n-L+1))
        cand[st:st+L] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=L))
        if rng.random()<0.2:
           cand *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma*0.5, size=n))
     else:
        idxs=rng.choice(n, size=min(n//10, 100), replace=False)
        cand[idxs]*=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma,size=idxs.size))
     cand=np.clip(cand,1e-12,None)
     mx=cand.max()
     if mx<=0: continue
     cand=cand/mx
     sc=score_fast(cand)
     if np.isfinite(sc):
        if sc < best_score*(1-1e-12): update; stagn=0; sigma*=0.9? maybe keep
        else: stagn+=1; if stagn>20: sigma=min(0.6,sigma*1.2); stagn=0
     it+=1
  return best

def normalize_sum_pos(a, floor=1e-12):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)

   return a

def basis_search(n, deadline, rng):
   q=get_q(n); x=...
   best=q/q.max(); best_score=score_fast(best)
   best_a=0.0; best_b=0.0
   # random
   while time.monotonic()<deadline:
      a=float(rng.uniform(-2.0,4.0))
      b=float(rng.uniform(-2.0,4.0))
      p=1.0 + a*x + b*x*x
      p=np.clip(p,0.0,None)
      f=q*p
      mx=f.max()
      if mx<=0: continue
      f=f/mx
      sc=score_fast(f)
      if sc<best_score: ...
   # coordinate refine
   step=0.5
   no=0
   for _ in range(2000):
      if time>deadline: break
      a2=best_a + rng.normal(0,step); b2=best_b + rng.normal(0,step)
      p=1+a2*x+b2*x*x; clip
      f=q*p; mx; sc
      if sc<best_score: update; step*=0.9; no=0
      else: no+=1; if no>20: step=min(2.0,step*1.3); no=0
   return best

```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum a)^2
for the provided evaluator.

The program combines:
  - a strong analytic seed from the binomial-square-root sequence q_i
  - a compact 2D polynomial perturbation search around q_i
  - several Frank-Wolfe-style LP best-response iterations (when scipy is
    available), using dense convolution matrices for moderate n
  - resampling to a final length and a short multiplicative local search

The best finite sequence found is returned, scaled so that all values are in
[0, 1000] (the evaluator clips to this range anyway).
"""

import numpy as np
import time

try:
    from scipy.optimize import linprog
    _HAVE_SCIPY = True
except Exception:
    _HAVE_SCIPY = False

_Q_CACHE = {}
_IDX_CACHE = {}


def get_q(n: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Return the first n coefficients of sqrt((1-x)^-1): C(2k,k)/4^k."""
    if n in _Q_CACHE:
        return _Q_CACHE[n].copy()
    q = np.empty(int(n), dtype=np.float64)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.empty(0, dtype=np.float64)
    q[0] = 1.0
    if n > 1:
        idx = np.arange(1, int(n), dtype=np.float64)
        # q_k / q_{k-1} = (2k - 1) / (2k)
        ratios = (2.0 * idx - 1.0) / (2.0 * idx)
        q[1:] = np.cumprod(ratios)
    _Q_CACHE[int(n)] = q
    return q.copy()


def normalize_sum_pos(a: np.ndarray, floor: float = 1.0e-12):
    """Normalize to sum 1 and make strictly positive (for LP stages)."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    a = a / s
    if a.size > 0:
        a = np.maximum(a, floor)
        s2 = float(a.sum())
        if s2 > 0.0:
            a = a / s2
    return a


def normalize_max_pos(a: np.ndarray, floor: float = 1.0e-12) -> np.ndarray:
    """Normalize to max 1 and make non-negative (for scoring/final candidates)."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    m = float(a.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(a.shape, dtype=np.float64)
    return a / m


def conv_max_fft(a: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """Fast max of the full linear convolution a*a via FFT."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return 0.0
    if n <= 96:
        return float(np.max(np.convolve(a, a)))
    m = 2 * n - 1
    L = 1
    while L < m:
        L <<= 1
    f = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(f * f, n=L)
    return float(np.max(c[:m]))


def score_fast(a: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """Fast evaluator surrogate (scale invariant)."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    mb = conv_max_fft(a)
    if not np.isfinite(mb) or mb < 0.0:
        mb = 0.0
    val = float(2.0 * n * mb / (s * s))
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        return float("inf")
    return val


def build_idx_info(n: int):
    """
    Precompute a dense convolution-matrix index layout.

    For the LP matrix A where A[k, j] = h[k-j] if valid, we store:
      valid[k, j] : True if k-j in [0, n-1]
      safe[k, j]  : valid index k-j, or 0 when invalid
    """
    if n in _IDX_CACHE:
        return _IDX_CACHE[n]
    rows = 2 * int(n) - 1
    idx = np.full((rows, int(n)), -1, dtype=np.int16 if n <= 32767 else np.int32)
    for k in range(rows):
        lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
        hi = min(n, k + 1)
        if hi > lo:
            idx[k, lo:hi] = k - np.arange(lo, hi)
    valid = idx >= 0
    safe = idx.copy()
    safe[~valid] = 0
    _IDX_CACHE[int(n)] = (valid, safe)
    return valid, safe


def solve_lp_best_response(f, valid, safe, safety: float = 1.02):
    """
    Given f (sum 1), solve:
        maximize sum g
        s.t. conv(f, g) <= max(conv(f, f))
             g >= 0
    by scaling the constraint with M and returning g normalized to sum 1.
    """
    if not _HAVE_SCIPY:
        return None
    f = np.asarray(f, dtype=np.float64)
    n = f.size
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    M = conv_max_fft(f)
    if not np.isfinite(M) or M <= 0.0:
        return None
    M *= safety
    h = f / M

    # A = h[valid indices]
    A = h[safe].copy()
    A[~valid] = 0.0

    c = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
    b_ub = np.ones(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
    bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n

    opts1 = {
        "presolve": True,
        "dual_feasibility": 1.0e-9,
        "primal_feasibility": 1.0e-9,
        "time_limit": 25.0,
    }
    try:
        res = linprog(c=c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds,
                      method="highs", options=opts1)
    except Exception:
        try:
            res = linprog(c=c, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=bounds,
                          method="highs", options={"presolve": True})
        except Exception:
            return None

    if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False):
        x = getattr(res, "x", None)
        if x is None:
            return None
        # Some solvers return a usable x with status != success.
    else:
        x = res.x

    if x is None:
        return None
    g = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
    g = np.clip(g, 0.0, None)
    s = float(g.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return None
    return g / s


def lp_stage(f0: np.ndarray, n: int, deadline: float, rng, label: str = ""):
    """
    Run best-response LP iterations at fixed length n.
    Returns (best_f_normalized_sum1, best_score).
    """
    f = normalize_sum_pos(f0.copy(), floor=1.0e-12)
    if f is None or f.size != n:
        q = get_q(n)
        f = normalize_sum_pos(q, floor=1.0e-12)

    cur_M = conv_max_fft(f)
    if not np.isfinite(cur_M) or cur_M <= 0.0:
        cur_M = 1.0 / n

    best_f = f.copy()
    best_M = cur_M
    best_score = float(2.0 * n * cur_M)
    current_time = time.monotonic()

    print(f"[LP {label}] n={n} start score={best_score:.7f} time={current_time:.2f}")

    valid, safe = build_idx_info(n)
    stagnation = 0
    failures = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline and it < 2000:
        it += 1
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break

        g = solve_lp_best_response(f, valid, safe, safety=1.02)
        if g is None:
            failures += 1
            if failures > 8:
                break
            continue
        failures = 0

        line_f = f
        line_M = cur_M
        for t in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0):
            fn = (1.0 - t) * f + t * g
            fn = normalize_sum_pos(fn, floor=1.0e-12)
            if fn is None or fn.size != n:
                continue
            Mn = conv_max_fft(fn)
            if np.isfinite(Mn) and Mn < line_M:
                line_M = Mn
                line_f = fn

        if line_M < cur_M * (1.0 - 1.0e-12):
            cur_M = line_M
            f = line_f
            stagnation = 0
            if cur_M < best_M:
                best_M = cur_M
                best_f = f.copy()
                best_score = float(2.0 * n * best_M)
                if it % 5 == 0:
                    print(f"[LP {label}] it={it} improved score={best_score:.7f} "
                          f"time={time.monotonic():.2f}")
        else:
            stagnation += 1
            if stagnation >= 4:
                # Perturb around current best to escape a local fixed point.
                sigma = 0.25 + 0.15 * min(6, stagnation)
                p = best_f * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=n))
                p = normalize_sum_pos(p, floor=1.0e-12)
                if p is not None:
                    f = 0.7 * p + 0.3 * best_f
                    f = normalize_sum_pos(f, floor=1.0e-12)
                    cur_M = conv_max_fft(f)
                    if not np.isfinite(cur_M) or cur_M <= 0.0:
                        f = best_f.copy()
                        cur_M = best_M
                    stagnation = 0
                    failures = min(failures, 3)

    print(f"[LP {label}] n={n} done it={it} best score={best_score:.7f}")
    return best_f, best_score


def resample_sequence(a: np.ndarray, n_new: int) -> np.ndarray:
    """Linearly resample a 1D nonnegative sequence to a new length."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n_old = a.size
    if n_old == 0:
        return np.zeros(max(1, int(n_new)), dtype=np.float64)
    if n_old == int(n_new):
        return a.copy()
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_old)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(n_new))
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a, left=0.0, right=0.0)
    return np.clip(out, 0.0, None)


def basis_search(n: int, deadline: float, rng):
    """
    Small 2D polynomial search around the q seed.
    Tries p(x)=1+a*x+b*x^2 with clipping to nonnegativity.
    """
    q = get_q(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    best = normalize_max_pos(q.copy())
    best_score = score_fast(best)
    best_a = 0.0
    best_b = 0.0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        a = float(rng.uniform(-2.0, 3.5))
        b = float(rng.uniform(-2.0, 3.5))
        p = 1.0 + a * x + b * x * x
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
        f = q * p
        mx = float(f.max())
        if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
            continue
        f = f / mx
        sc = score_fast(f)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best = f
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a
            best_b = b

    # Coordinate refinement
    step = 0.6
    no_improve = 0
    for _ in range(2500):
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        a2 = best_a + float(rng.normal(0.0, step))
        b2 = best_b + float(rng.normal(0.0, step))
        p = 1.0 + a2 * x + b2 * x * x
        p = np.clip(p, 0.0, None)
        f = q * p
        mx = float(f.max())
        if mx <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(mx):
            continue
        f = f / mx
        sc = score_fast(f)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score * (1.0 - 1.0e-13):
            best = f
            best_score = sc
            best_a = a2
            best_b = b2
            step = max(0.05, step * 0.85)
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve > 25:
                step = min(2.5, step * 1.25)
                no_improve = 0

    print(f"[basis] n={n} best score={best_score:.7f}")
    return best


def local_search(a: np.ndarray, deadline: float, rng):
    """
    Short multiplicative block local search using fast FFT scores.
    """
    cur = normalize_max_pos(a.copy())
    best = cur.copy()
    best_score = score_fast(best)
    sigma = 0.12
    stagnation = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        n = cur.size
        cand = cur.copy()

        if rng.random() < 0.75:
            lo_len = max(2, n // 250)
            hi_len = max(lo_len + 1, n // 18)
            length = int(rng.integers(lo_len, hi_len + 1))
            start = int(rng.integers(0, max(1, n - length + 1)))
            stop = min(n, start + length)
            cand[start:stop] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=stop - start))
            if rng.random() < 0.15:
                cand *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.5 * sigma))
        else:
            k = max(3, min(n // 12, 200))
            idx = rng.choice(n, size=k, replace=False)
            cand[idx] *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, size=k))

        cand = np.clip(cand, 1.0e-12, None)
        mx = float(cand.max())
        if not np.isfinite(mx) or mx <= 0.0:
            continue
        cand = cand / mx
        sc = score_fast(cand)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score * (1.0 - 1.0e-12):
            best = cand
            best_score = sc
            cur = cand
            stagnation = 0
            sigma = max(0.03, sigma * 0.92)
            if it % 200 == 0:
                print(f"[local] it={it} score={best_score:.7f}")
        else:
            stagnation += 1
            if stagnation > 30:
                sigma = min(0.6, sigma * 1.18)
                stagnation = 0

    print(f"[local] n={cur.size} final score={best_score:.7f}")
    return best


def scale_for_evaluator(a: np.ndarray, max_value: float = 1000.0) -> list:
    """Scale a candidate to the evaluator's allowed range and return a Python list."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, None)
    m = float(a.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 0.0:
        # Uniform fallback (coarse but valid)
        out = np.full(a.size if a.size > 0 else 64, 1.0, dtype=np.float64)
    else:
        out = a / m * float(max_value)
    out = np.clip(out, 0.0, float(max_value))
    return [float(v) for v in out]


def authoritative_score(seq: list):
    """Use the given evaluate_sequence if present; otherwise fall back to FFT score."""
    try:
        s = float(evaluate_sequence(seq))  # noqa: F821 (assumed injected)
        return s
    except Exception:
        arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
        return score_fast(arr)


def propose_candidate(seed: int = 42, budget_s: float = 2400.0, **kwargs):
    total = max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    start = time.monotonic()
    final_deadline = start + max(30.0, total - 45.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    candidates = []
    candidate_notes = []

    # A safe final length: enough for asymptotics, small enough for np.convolve.
    N_FINAL = 3072

    # Base q seed at final length.
    q_final = normalize_max_pos(get_q(N_FINAL).copy())
    candidates.append(q_final)
    candidate_notes.append("q_seed_final")

    # Very short basis search may give a better shape.
    basis_end = min(final_deadline, start + 90.0)
    if time.monotonic() < basis_end:
        N_basis = 512
        b_best = basis_search(N_basis, basis_end, rng)
        if b_best is not None and b_best.size > 0:
            candidates.append(resample_sequence(b_best, N_FINAL))
            candidate_notes.append("basis_resampled")

    # LP Frank-Wolfe stages if available.
    local_reserve = 420.0
    lp_deadline = min(final_deadline, start + max(0.0, total - local_reserve - 120.0))

    if _HAVE_SCIPY and time.monotonic() < lp_deadline:
        N_LP1 = 512
        stage1_end = min(lp_deadline, start + 520.0)
        if time.monotonic() < stage1_end:
            f1 = normalize_sum_pos(get_q(N_LP1).copy(), floor=1.0e-12)
            best1, score1 = lp_stage(f1, N_LP1, stage1_end, rng, label="stage1")
            if best1 is not None:
                candidates.append(resample_sequence(best1, N_FINAL))
                candidate_notes.append(f"lp1_score={score1:.7f}")

        N_LP2 = 1024
        if time.monotonic() < lp_deadline:
            f2 = None
            if best1 is not None:
                f2 = resample_sequence(best1, N_LP2)
            if f2 is None or f2.size != N_LP2:
                f2 = resample_sequence(get_q(N_LP2), N_LP2)
            f2 = normalize_sum_pos(f2, floor=1.0e-12)
            best2, score2 = lp_stage(f2, N_LP2, lp_deadline, rng, label="stage2")
            if best2 is not None:
                candidates.append(resample_sequence(best2, N_FINAL))
                candidate_notes.append(f"lp2_score={score2:.7f}")

    # Final local search from the currently best candidate (by fast score).
    local_start = time.monotonic()
    if local_start < final_deadline:
        # Pick the best candidate by fast score for local refinement.
        best_arr = q_final
        best_sc = score_fast(best_arr)
        for arr in candidates:
            arrm = normalize_max_pos(arr.copy())
            sc = score_fast(arrm)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
                best_sc = sc
                best_arr = arrm
        print(f"[local] starting from score={best_sc:.7f}")
        poor = local_search(best_arr, final_deadline, rng)
        if poor is not None:
            candidates.append(poor)
            candidate_notes.append("local_refined")

    # Choose the best candidate using the authoritative evaluator when available.
    best_seq = scale_for_evaluator(q_final, 1000.0)
    best_val = authoritative_score(best_seq)

    seen = set()
    for idx, arr in enumerate(candidates):
        try:
            seq = scale_for_evaluator(arr, 1000.0)
            key = (len(seq), seq[0] if seq else 0.0, seq[-1] if seq else 0.0)
            if key in seen:
                continue
            seen.add(key)
        except Exception:
            continue
        try:
            sc = authoritative_score(seq)
        except Exception:
            sc = float("inf")
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_val:
            best_val = sc
            best_seq = seq

    # Final sanity: if the authoritative score is bad but we have a healthy q seed,
    # fall back to the q seed scaled by the evaluator.
    if not np.isfinite(best_val) or best_val > 2.0:
        fallback = scale_for_evaluator(q_final, 1000.0)
        fallback_sc = authoritative_score(fallback)
        if np.isfinite(fallback_sc) and fallback_sc < best_val:
            best_seq = fallback
            best_val = fallback_sc

    print(f"[final] returned length={len(best_seq)} score={best_val:.7f}")
    return [float(x) for x in best_seq]
```
